### BitrateSwitchingEvents

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| ~~`activeLayer`~~ | `null` \| [`BitrateLayer`](types.mdx#bitratelayer) | **Deprecated** Use `currentQuality` instead |
| `availableQualities` | [`Quality`](types.mdx#quality)[] | **Description** Is emitted when the array of availableQualities is set/changes. **Example** `player.on("availableQualities", (arr) => { // display available qualities})` |
| `blurred?` | `boolean` | - |
| `currentQuality` | `null` \| [`Quality`](types.mdx#quality) | **Description** Is emitted when the currentQuality is updated. **Example** `player.on("currentQuality", (val) => { // handle quality update})` |
| ~~`layers`~~ | [`BitrateLayer`](types.mdx#bitratelayer)[] | **Deprecated** Use `availableQualities` instead |

### BroadcastAPI

Represents a broadcast and ability to control it

#### Extends

- [`Merge`](types.mdx#mergea-b-c)\<`IEventEmitter`\<[`BroadcastEvents`](types.mdx#broadcastevents)\>, [`Disposable`](types.mdx#disposable)\>.`Serializable`

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `audioMuted` | `boolean` | Changing that flag to true disables all audio tracks and broadcast that information to all peers **Emits** audioMuted when audioMuted has changed |
| `isDisposed` | `boolean` | - |
| `state` | `"closed"` \| `"paused"` \| `"error"` \| `"active"` \| `"stall"` | Broadcast state. **Emits** state when broadcast state has changed |
| `streamName` | `string` | - |
| `videoHidden` | `boolean` | Changing that flag to true disables all video tracks and broadcast that information to all peers **Emits** videoHidden when videoHidden has changed |

### BroadcastingEvents

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `isConnected` | `boolean` | **Description** // emitted when the websocket is connected or disconnected **Example** `player.on("isConnected", (bool) => { if(!bool) { // handle call connected} })` |
| `isConnecting` | `boolean` | **Description** // emitted when the websocket is connecting and when finished connecting **Example** `player.on("isConnecting", (bool) => { if(!bool) { // handle call connecting } })` |
| `websocketReconnect` | `void` | **Description** // emitted when the websocket is going to reconnect **Example** `player.on("websocketReconnect", (bool) => { if(!bool) { // handle call websocketReconnect} })` |

### CallAPI

#### Extends

- [`Merge`](types.mdx#mergea-b-c)\<`IEventEmitter`\<[`CallEvents`](types.mdx#callevents)\>, [`Disposable`](types.mdx#disposable)\>.`Serializable`

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `broadcasts` | `Map`\<`string`, [`BroadcastAPI`](types.mdx#broadcastapi)\> | Array of call broadcasts. **Emits** broadcasts - triggers when a broadcast has changed, been added, or removed. |
| `id` | `string` | A call ID. Can be used to join the call with `joinCall()` method **See** VideoClient.joinCall |
| `isDisposed` | `boolean` | - |
| `isOwner` | `boolean` | Indicates the owner of the call |
| `peers` | [`PeerAPI`](types.mdx#peerapi)[] | A list of connected peers **Emits** mediaAdded - triggers when a new peer added **Emits** mediaRemoved - triggers when a old peer removed |
| `state` | `"closed"` \| `"error"` \| `"active"` \| `"stall"` | Represents a current call state **Emits** state - triggers when state has changed |

### ConsumerEvents

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `consumerAudioEnabled` | `boolean` | **Description** Is emitted when the remote peer's audio track active state changes. **Example** `player.on("consumerAudioEnabled", (bool) => { if(!bool) { // handle no audio } })` |
| `consumerVideoEnabled` | `boolean` | **Description** Is emitted when the remote peer's video track active state changes. **Example** `player.on("consumerVideoEnabled", (bool) => { if(!bool) { // handle no video } })` |
| `noConsumerAudioAndVideo` | `boolean` | **Description** Is emitted when the remote peer's audio or video track active state changes. **Example** `player.on("noConsumerAudioAndVideo", (bool) => { if(!bool) { // handle no audio and video } })` |

***

### ConsumerFeature

#### Extends

- `IEventEmitter`\<[`ConsumerEvents`](types.mdx#consumerevents)\>

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `consumerAudioEnabled` | `boolean` | Indicates that the remote peer has an active audio track |
| `consumerVideoEnabled` | `boolean` | Indicates that the remote peer has an active video track |
| `noConsumerAudioAndVideo` | `boolean` | Indicates that the remote peer has no active audio or video track |
| `streamName` | `string` | - |

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `forcedMute` | `boolean` | **Description** Is emitted when a video has to be played muted because of low media engagement on the site **Example** `player.on("forcedMute", (bool) => { if(bool) { // show eye-catching UI to force user interaction } })` |

### PeerAPI

Represents a remote connected user

#### Extends

- [`Merge`](types.mdx#mergea-b-c)\<`IEventEmitter`\<[`PeerEvents`](types.mdx#peerevents)\>, [`Disposable`](types.mdx#disposable)\>.`Serializable`

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `appData` | `Record`\<`string`, `unknown`\> | Any metadata passed by remote peer E.g. user's displayName |
| `call?` | [`CallAPI`](types.mdx#callapi) | A reference to current call Can be null if that's cam2cam activity |
| `isDisposed` | `boolean` | - |
| `peerId?` | `string` | Unique ID of this peer Can be null if that's cam2cam activity |
| `streams` | [`SourceProvider`](types.mdx#sourceprovidersource)\<[`MediaStream`](adapter.mdx#mediastream)\>[] | A list of active streams |
| `userId?` | `string` | Unique user ID Can be null if that's cam2cam activity |

### PlayerAPI\<HostElement\>

Represents a media player which controls VideoElement

#### Extends

- [`Merge`](types.mdx#mergea-b-c)\<`IEventEmitter`\<[`PlayerEvents`](types.mdx#playereventshostelement)\>, [`Disposable`](types.mdx#disposable)\>.`Serializable`

#### Type Parameters

| Type Parameter | Default type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `HostElement` *extends* [`VideoElement`](types.mdx#videoelement) | [`VideoElement`](types.mdx#videoelement) |

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `attached?` | `boolean` | Returns true if VideoElement is attached |
| `autoPlay?` | `boolean` | If true then the player will start playing as manifest loaded Otherwise it will wait for a user interaction |
| ~~`driverFailover`~~ | `boolean` | **Deprecated** This is not exposed anymore and will be removed completely in the future **Throws** |
| `format?` | `null` \| keyof ManifestFormats | Returns a current driver format |
| `isDisposed` | `boolean` | - |
| `localAudioMuted?` | `boolean` | True if local audio is muted If WebRTC is used then it also stops all audio traffics **Emits** localAudioMuted if localAudioMuted has changed |
| `localAudioVolume?` | `number` | Current local volume **Emits** localAudioVolume if localAudioVolume has changed |
| `localVideoPaused?` | `boolean` | True if the player is disposed |
| ~~`lowPowerMode?`~~ | `boolean` | **Deprecated** Not used anymore **Throws** |
| `poster?` | `null` \| `string` | URL to custom poster image which shows before the video is played |

### CallEvents

> **CallEvents**: `object`

#### Type declaration

##### accessDenied

> **accessDenied**: `object`

###### Description

Is emitted when an accessToken is used more than once.

###### Example

```ts
call.on("accessDenied", () => { // do something })
```

##### accessDenied.message

> **message**: `string`

##### callClosed

> **callClosed**: `object`

###### Description

Is emitted when the call is closed.

###### Example

```ts
call.on("peerRemoved", ({callId}) => { // handle cleanup })
```

##### callClosed.callId

> **callId**: `string`

##### callEnded

> **callEnded**: `void`

###### Description

Is emitted when the call is ended by the owner.

###### Example

```ts
call.on("callEnded", () => { // do something })
```

##### callId

> **callId**: `object`

###### Description

Is emitted when the callId is set.

###### Example

```ts
call.on("callId", ({callId, user}) => { // do something with callId and user info})
```

##### callId.callId

> **callId**: `string`

##### callId.user

> **user**: [`SFUJoinUser`](types.mdx#sfujoinuser) \| `null`

##### callJoinEndpoint

> **callJoinEndpoint**: `void`

##### disposed

> **disposed**: `void`

###### Description

Is emitted when the call is disposed.

```ts
call.on("disposed", () => { // handle dispose })
```

##### dominantSpeaker

> **dominantSpeaker**: [`DominantSpeaker`](types.mdx#dominantspeaker)

###### Description

Is emitted when the dominantSpeaker changes.

###### Example

```ts
call.on("dominantSpeaker", (speaker) => { // do something with the speaker })
```

##### error

```ts
call.on("error", (err) => { // handle err })
```

##### streamRemoved.streamName

> **streamName**: `string`

##### transportStateChange

> **transportStateChange**: `object`

Is emitted when websocket state has changed

Possible states:
  notConnected - the connection is not opened yet or successfully finished
                 in this state it will not attempt to reconnect
  connecting   - the websocket connection is opened but authorization is not finished,
                 the transport is not ready yet
  connected    - the connection is successfully established and authorized
  suspended    - all transport work is temporary suspended (usually due loosing network)
  closed       - the connection was closed by some reason
                 the transport may attempt to reconnect automatically

##### transportStateChange.state

> **state**: `"notConnected"` \| `"connecting"` \| `"connected"` \| `"suspended"` \| `"closed"`

##### videoConsumer

> **videoConsumer**: `void`

##### viewerKicked

> **viewerKicked**: `void`

###### Description

Is emitted when the a viewer is kicked from a private stream.

###### Example

```ts
call.on("viewerKicked", () => { // do something })
```

##### websocketReconnect

> **websocketReconnect**: `void`

###### Description

Is emitted when the call websocket is going to reconnect.

###### Example

```ts
call.on("websocketReconnect", () => { // handle reconnect })
```

##### zeroBitrate

> **zeroBitrate**: [`BroadcastEvents`](types.mdx#broadcastevents)\[`"zeroBitrate"`\]

```ts
mediaController.on("aspectRatio", (val) => {  if(val){ // do something with aspectRatio }})
```

### PlayerEvents\<HostElement\>

> **PlayerEvents**\<`HostElement`\>: `object`

#### Type Parameters

| Type Parameter | Default type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `HostElement` *extends* [`VideoElement`](types.mdx#videoelement) | [`VideoElement`](types.mdx#videoelement) |

#### Type declaration

##### availableQualities

> **availableQualities**: [`Quality`](types.mdx#quality)[]

###### Description

Is emitted when the player is disposed.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("disposed", () => { //do some cleanup})
```

##### blurred?

> `optional` **blurred**: `boolean`

###### Description

Is emitted when a stream is either blurred or not blurred.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("blurred", () => { // do something)
```

##### callEnded

> **callEnded**: `void`

###### Description

Is emitted when a call has been ended by the owner.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("callEnded", () => { // do something})
```

##### consumerAudioEnabled

> **consumerAudioEnabled**: `boolean`

###### Description

Is emitted when the remote peer's audio track active state changes.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("consumerAudioEnabled", (bool) => { if(!bool) { // handle no audio } })
```

##### consumerVideoEnabled

> **consumerVideoEnabled**: `boolean`

###### Description

Is emitted when the remote peer's video track active state changes.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("consumerVideoEnabled", (bool) => { if(!bool) { // handle no video } })
```

##### currentQuality

> **currentQuality**: [`Quality`](types.mdx#quality) \| `null`

##### disposed

> **disposed**: `void`

##### driver

> **driver**: `string`

###### Description

Emits the current driver in use by a manifestPlayer

###### Example

```ts
player.on("driver", (driver) => { console.log(driver)})
```

##### driverFailover

> **driverFailover**: `boolean`

###### Description

Is emitted when a timeout has occurred and driverFailover is true.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("driverFailover", () => { // switch to next driver)
```

##### error

> **error**: [`AnyError`](types.mdx#anyerror)

##### forcedMute

> **forcedMute**: `boolean`

###### Description

Is emitted when an autoplaying player has been forced to mute by the browser.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("forcedMute", () => { // show unmute button)
```

##### hostElementAttached

> **hostElementAttached**: `object`

###### Description

Is emitted when the HTMLVideoElement supplied receives a src or srcObject.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("hostElementAttached", ({el}) => { // display element})
```

##### hostElementAttached.el

> **el**: `HostElement`

##### implementation

> **implementation**: `void`

Is emitted when the player changes its implementation.

##### joinedCall

> **joinedCall**: `object`

###### Description

Is emitted after the call joins in a webrtc stream.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("joinedCall", ({ call }) => { // handle call })
```

##### joinedCall.call

> **call**: [`CallAPI`](types.mdx#callapi)

##### localAudioMuted

> **localAudioMuted**: `boolean`

###### Description

Is emitted when the audio is muted.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("localAudioMuted", (bool) => { if(bool) { // show a no-audio icon } })
```

##### localAudioVolume

> **localAudioVolume**: `number`

###### Description

Is emitted when the player volume is changed.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("localAudioVolume", (number) => { //set volume range to number})
```

##### localVideoPaused

> **localVideoPaused**: `boolean`

###### Description

Is emitted when the video is paused.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("localVideoPaused", (bool) => { if(bool) { // show a paused icon } })
```

##### peerAtCapacity

> **peerAtCapacity**: `boolean`

###### Description

Is emitted when a private stream is at peer capacity for producers.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("peerAtCapacity", (bool) => { if(!bool) { // handle it } })
```

##### playerAccessDenied

> **playerAccessDenied**: `object`

###### Description

Is emitted when an accessToken is used more than once.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("playerAccessDenied", () => { // do something })
```

##### playerAccessDenied.message

> **message**: `string`

##### progress

> **progress**: `void`

###### Description

Is emitted as the player progresses.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("progress", () => { console.log("progress")})
```

##### restartDriver

> **restartDriver**: `object`

##### restartDriver.timeout

> **timeout**: `number`

##### ~~timeupdate~~

> **timeupdate**: `void`

###### Deprecated

Use `timeupdate` on video element instead

###### Description

Is emitted when the player's time updates.

###### Example

```ts
player.on("timeupdate", () => { console.log("timeupdate")})
```

##### videoFirstPlay

> **videoFirstPlay**: `void`

###### Description

Is emitted when the player is played for the first time.

###### Example

```ts
player.once("videoFirstPlay", () => { //do some cleanup})
```
